main.py
"""
ReelsForge — main entry point.
Запуск: python main.py
Или собрать в exe:  python -m nuitka --standalone --enable-plugin=pyqt6 --windows-console-mode=disable main.py
"""
import sys, os, asyncio
from pathlib import Path

# Windows: правильная политика event loop для asyncio + Qt
if sys.platform == "win32":
    asyncio.set_event_loop_policy(asyncio.WindowsSelectorEventLoopPolicy())

# Убедимся, что ffmpeg найдётся (положи ffmpeg.exe рядом с exe или в PATH)
_bin = Path(__file__).parent / "bin"
if _bin.exists():
    os.environ["PATH"] = str(_bin) + os.pathsep + os.environ["PATH"]

# папки под данные
for d in ("data", "logs", "cache", "assets"):
    Path(d).mkdir(exist_ok=True)

# devices.json — если нет, создадим стартовый
_devices_file = Path("assets/devices.json")
if not _devices_file.exists():
    from assets.gen_devices import generate_starter_pool
    generate_starter_pool(_devices_file)


def main():
    from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QApplication
    import qasync, concurrent.futures
    from gui.main_window import MainWindow

    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    app.setApplicationName("ReelsForge")
    app.setStyle("Fusion")

    loop = qasync.QEventLoop(app)
    asyncio.set_event_loop(loop)

    # чтобы _sleep через executor не упирался в дефолтные 32 воркера
    loop.set_default_executor(
        concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=256)
    )

    w = MainWindow()
    w.show()

    with loop:
        loop.run_forever()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

covers.py
"""
Приводит все картинки из папки к формату Reels cover: 1080x1920 JPG.
- Multiprocessing по всем ядрам CPU (в 6-10 раз быстрее)
- Пропускает уже готовые файлы
- Прогресс-бар

Использование:
    py covers.py                        # raw_covers/ -> covers/
    py covers.py raw_covers/ covers/    # явно указать папки
"""
import sys
import os
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image
from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed
from functools import partial

try:
    from tqdm import tqdm
except ImportError:
    print("Ставлю tqdm...")
    os.system(f'"{sys.executable}" -m pip install tqdm')
    from tqdm import tqdm


SRC = Path(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "raw_covers")
DST = Path(sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "covers")

TARGET_W, TARGET_H = 1080, 1920
QUALITY = 88

DST.mkdir(exist_ok=True)


def process_one(src_file: Path, dst_dir: Path, target_w: int,
                target_h: int, quality: int):
    """Обрабатывает один файл. Возвращает (status, msg)."""
    out = dst_dir / f"{src_file.stem}.jpg"

    if out.exists():
        return ("skip", src_file.name)

    try:
        img = Image.open(src_file).convert("RGB")
        w, h = img.size

        target_ratio = target_w / target_h
        current_ratio = w / h

        # crop под 9:16
        if current_ratio > target_ratio:
            new_w = int(h * target_ratio)
            left = (w - new_w) // 2
            img = img.crop((left, 0, left + new_w, h))
        elif current_ratio < target_ratio:
            new_h = int(w / target_ratio)
            top = (h - new_h) // 2
            img = img.crop((0, top, w, top + new_h))

        # LANCZOS для downscale (качество), BILINEAR для upscale (скорость)
        resample = Image.LANCZOS if img.size[0] > target_w else Image.BILINEAR
        img = img.resize((target_w, target_h), resample)

        # optimize=False + progressive=False — save в разы быстрее
        img.save(out, format="JPEG", quality=quality, optimize=False, progressive=False)
        return ("ok", src_file.name)
    except Exception as e:
        return ("err", f"{src_file.name}: {e}")


def main():
    if not SRC.exists():
        print(f"Папка не существует: {SRC}")
        return

    # собираем все файлы
    files_set = set()
    for ext in ("*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.png", "*.webp"):
        files_set.update(SRC.glob(ext))
    files = list(files_set)

    if not files:
        print(f"Нет картинок в {SRC}")
        return

    # быстрая предфильтрация уже готовых
    existing = {p.stem for p in DST.glob("*.jpg")}
    todo = [f for f in files if f.stem not in existing]
    skipped_pre = len(files) - len(todo)

    print(f"=======================================")
    print(f"Источник:     {SRC}")
    print(f"Назначение:   {DST}")
    print(f"Формат:       {TARGET_W}x{TARGET_H} (cover 9:16)")
    print(f"=======================================")
    print(f"Всего файлов: {len(files)}")
    print(f"Уже готово:   {skipped_pre}")
    print(f"К обработке:  {len(todo)}")

    if not todo:
        print("\nВсё уже готово, нечего делать.")
        return

    workers = max(2, (os.cpu_count() or 4) - 1)
    print(f"Воркеров:     {workers}")
    print(f"=======================================\n")

    ok = 0
    errors = []

    worker = partial(process_one, dst_dir=DST, target_w=TARGET_W,
                     target_h=TARGET_H, quality=QUALITY)

    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers) as ex:
        futures = [ex.submit(worker, f) for f in todo]
        for fut in tqdm(as_completed(futures), total=len(todo),
                       desc="Обработка", unit="img"):
            status, msg = fut.result()
            if status == "ok":
                ok += 1
            elif status == "err":
                errors.append(msg)

    print(f"\n=======================================")
    print(f"Обработано:            {ok}")
    print(f"Пропущено (уже было):  {skipped_pre}")
    print(f"Ошибок:                {len(errors)}")
    print(f"Итого в {DST}/:  ~{ok + skipped_pre}")
    print(f"=======================================")

    if errors:
        print(f"\nПервые 5 ошибок:")
        for e in errors[:5]:
            print(f"  {e}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

add_text.py (не юзаю часто так как 10к картинок и куча действий, по типу качать кидать их в raw_covers, потом через это и лишь потом в коверс нужным форматом)
"""
Добавляет случайный текст-хук на обложки.
Пропускай папку через это ПЕРЕД тем как заливать в софт.
"""
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
import random
from pathlib import Path
import os

# ─── топ CTR-хуки для spiritual/cinematic ниши ───
HOOKS = [
    # призывы к действию
    "WATCH THIS",
    "WAIT FOR IT",
    "LOOK AGAIN",
    "SEE THIS",
    "READ THIS",
    # curiosity gap
    "THE TRUTH",
    "SECRET",
    "REVEALED",
    "YOU MISSED THIS",
    "HIDDEN",
    # emotional
    "GOD IS REAL",
    "THIS WILL CHANGE YOU",
    "SIGN FROM UNIVERSE",
    "SYNCHRONICITY",
    "AWAKENING",
    "1111",
    "222",
    "444",
    "IT'S TIME",
    "TRUST YOUR PATH",
    # curiosity questions
    "WHAT IF?",
    "DID YOU KNOW?",
    "REALLY?",
    "TRUE STORY",
    # spiritual
    "THIRD EYE OPEN",
    "SHIFT HAPPENING",
    "COSMIC SIGN",
]

INPUT_DIR = Path("raw_covers")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("covers_with_text")
OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

# путь к шрифту (Impact в Windows системный)
FONT_PATH = "C:/Windows/Fonts/impact.ttf"

def add_hook(img_path: Path):
    try:
        img = Image.open(img_path).convert("RGB")
        w, h = img.size

        # ресайз к 1080x1920 если нужно (9:16)
        target_ratio = 9 / 16
        if abs(w/h - target_ratio) > 0.05:
            # crop к 9:16
            new_w = int(h * target_ratio)
            if new_w <= w:
                left = (w - new_w) // 2
                img = img.crop((left, 0, left + new_w, h))
            else:
                new_h = int(w / target_ratio)
                top = (h - new_h) // 2
                img = img.crop((0, top, w, top + new_h))

        img = img.resize((1080, 1920), Image.LANCZOS)

        draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
        hook = random.choice(HOOKS)

        # размер шрифта пропорционален длине текста
        font_size = min(180, int(2000 / max(len(hook), 4)))
        font = ImageFont.truetype(FONT_PATH, size=font_size)

        # позиция — верхняя треть, случайно смещённая
        bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), hook, font=font)
        text_w = bbox[2] - bbox[0]
        text_h = bbox[3] - bbox[1]

        x = (1080 - text_w) // 2
        y = random.randint(int(1920 * 0.10), int(1920 * 0.25))

        # обводка чёрная (толстая)
        outline_width = 6
        for dx in range(-outline_width, outline_width + 1, 2):
            for dy in range(-outline_width, outline_width + 1, 2):
                if dx == 0 and dy == 0:
                    continue
                draw.text((x + dx, y + dy), hook, font=font, fill="black")

        # основной текст — жёлтый или белый (лучший CTR)
        color = random.choice(["#FFD500", "#FFFFFF", "#FF3333"])
        draw.text((x, y), hook, font=font, fill=color)

        out = OUTPUT_DIR / img_path.name
        img.save(out, "JPEG", quality=92)
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[skip] {img_path.name}: {e}")
        return False


def main():
    count = 0
    files = [f for ext in ("*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.png", "*.webp")
             for f in INPUT_DIR.glob(ext)]
    print(f"Найдено {len(files)} файлов для обработки")

    for f in files:
        if add_hook(f):
            count += 1
            if count % 100 == 0:
                print(f"  обработано: {count}")

    print(f"\nГотово: {count} обложек с текстом → {OUTPUT_DIR}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

core/uniquifier.py
"""
Уникализатор с семафором на ffmpeg (защита от перегрузки CPU при 70+ потоках).
Универсальный пресет = быстрый + реально ломает pHash + чистит метаданные.
"""
import subprocess
import random
import tempfile
import os
import asyncio
from pathlib import Path


# Семафор для параллельных ffmpeg — независим от network threads
# 6 одновременных ffmpeg = ~90% загрузка 6-8 ядерного CPU
_FFMPEG_SEM = asyncio.Semaphore(12)


class ReelUniquifier:
    """
    Уровни:
        fast       — только remux + metadata strip (~0.5 сек, слабое изменение pHash)
        universal  — trim + micro-crop + audio pitch + metadata strip (~2-4 сек, СИЛЬНОЕ изменение pHash)
        light      — как universal но нежнее (~3-5 сек)
        medium     — universal + eq + rotate (~5-8 сек)
        heavy      — full processing, лучше не юзать на 70 потоков (~10-15 сек)
    """

    @staticmethod
    async def unique_async(src: str, dst: str = None, level: str = "universal",
                           audio: bool = True) -> str:
        """Async обёртка с семафором. Используй эту в orchestrator."""
        async with _FFMPEG_SEM:
            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
            return await loop.run_in_executor(
                None, ReelUniquifier.unique, src, dst, level, audio
            )

    @staticmethod
    def unique(src: str, dst: str = None, level: str = "universal",
               audio: bool = True) -> str:
        """
        Основной диспетчер уровней уникализации.
        Если доступен NVENC (NVIDIA GPU) — universal автоматически идёт через GPU.
        """
        dst = dst or tempfile.mktemp(suffix=".mp4")

        # ── авто GPU для universal, если есть NVENC (без параметра use_gpu) ──
        if level == "universal" and ReelUniquifier._has_nvenc():
            return ReelUniquifier._universal_nvenc(src, dst, audio)

        if level == "fast":
            return ReelUniquifier._fast(src, dst)
        if level == "compact":
            return ReelUniquifier._compact(src, dst, audio)
        if level == "universal":
            return ReelUniquifier._universal(src, dst, audio)
        if level == "light":
            return ReelUniquifier._light(src, dst, audio)
        if level == "medium":
            return ReelUniquifier._medium(src, dst, audio)
        if level == "heavy":
            return ReelUniquifier._heavy(src, dst, audio)

        return ReelUniquifier._universal(src, dst, audio)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────
    @staticmethod
    def _random_encoder_signature():
        """Случайные метаданные — маскируем что это ffmpeg."""
        return {
            "encoder": random.choice([
                "Lavc60.31.102 libx264",       # ffmpeg
                "Apple H.264 Encoder",         # iPhone
                "Android MediaCodec",          # Android
                "InstagramAndroid 449.0.0",
                "InstagramiOS 449.0.0",
            ]),
            "comment": os.urandom(8).hex(),
            "title": f"video_{random.randint(100000, 999999)}",
            "software": random.choice([
                "Instagram", "com.instagram.android", "iOS Photos",
            ]),
        }
    @staticmethod
    def _has_nvenc() -> bool:
        """Проверяет доступность NVENC (NVIDIA GPU encoder)."""
        if hasattr(ReelUniquifier, "_nvenc_cache"):
            return ReelUniquifier._nvenc_cache
        try:
            out = subprocess.check_output(
                ["ffmpeg", "-hide_banner", "-encoders"],
                stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, timeout=5
            ).decode(errors="ignore")
            has = "h264_nvenc" in out
            ReelUniquifier._nvenc_cache = has
            return has
        except Exception:
            ReelUniquifier._nvenc_cache = False
            return False
    @staticmethod
    def _run_ffmpeg(cmd: list, description: str = "ffmpeg") -> None:
        """
        Запускает ffmpeg и при ошибке выводит РЕАЛЬНЫЙ stderr,
        а не проглатывает его.
        """
        try:
            subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True, timeout=180)
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
            stderr = ""
            if e.stderr:
                stderr = e.stderr.decode(errors='ignore').strip()
            # берём последние 400 символов — там обычно причина
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"[{description}] ffmpeg failed: {stderr[-400:]}"
            ) from e
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            raise RuntimeError(f"[{description}] ffmpeg timeout > 180s")

    @staticmethod
    def _universal_nvenc(src, dst, audio):
        """
        NVENC-ускоренная версия. При ошибке fallback на CPU-версию.
        """
        speed = random.uniform(0.99, 1.01)
        trim_start = random.uniform(0.10, 0.25)
        crop = random.randint(2, 6)
        audio_semitones = random.uniform(-0.15, 0.15)

        vf = (
            f"trim=start={trim_start},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
            f"crop=iw-{crop*2}:ih-{crop*2},"
            f"scale=720:1280:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
            f"pad=720:1280:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2:color=black,"
            f"setpts={1/speed}*PTS"
        )

        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
               "-i", src, "-vf", vf]

        if audio:
            audio_speed = speed * (2 ** (audio_semitones / 12))
            af = (
                f"atrim=start={trim_start},asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
                f"asetrate=44100*{2**(audio_semitones/12)},aresample=44100,"
                f"atempo={audio_speed / (2**(audio_semitones/12))}"
            )
            cmd += ["-af", af, "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "96k"]
        else:
            cmd += ["-an"]

        meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
        cmd += [
            "-c:v", "h264_nvenc",
            "-preset", "p4",
            "-rc", "vbr",
            "-cq", "26",
            "-b:v", "0",
            "-profile:v", "high",
            "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
            "-g", str(random.choice([48, 60, 72])),
            "-t", "60",
            "-map_metadata", "-1",
            "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
            "-metadata", f"comment={meta['comment']}",
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            dst,
        ]

        try:
            ReelUniquifier._run_ffmpeg(cmd, "universal-nvenc")
            return dst
        except RuntimeError as e:
            # NVENC-ошибка → отключаем на будущее + fallback на CPU
            print(f"[uniquify] NVENC failed, fallback to CPU: {str(e)[:200]}")
            ReelUniquifier._nvenc_cache = False   # больше не пытаемся GPU
            return ReelUniquifier._universal(src, dst, audio)
    # ─────────────────────────────────────
    @staticmethod
    def _fast(src, dst):
        """Только remux + metadata strip. Быстро но pHash слабо меняется."""
        meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
        cmd = [
            "ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
            "-i", src,
            "-c", "copy",
            "-map_metadata", "-1",           # убираем ВСЕ метаданные из инпута
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
            "-metadata", f"comment={meta['comment']}",
            "-metadata", f"title={meta['title']}",
            dst,
        ]
        ReelUniquifier._run_ffmpeg(cmd, "fast")   # или "compact"/"light"/"medium"/"heavy"
        return dst

    # ─────────────────────────────────────
    @staticmethod
    def _universal(src, dst, audio):
        """
        Universal с обработкой ошибок и fallback:
        - Если аудио-фильтр падает (нет аудио дорожки) — retry без -af
        - Ошибки ffmpeg показываются с реальным stderr
        """
        speed = random.uniform(0.99, 1.01)
        trim_start = random.uniform(0.10, 0.25)
        crop = random.randint(2, 6)
        audio_semitones = random.uniform(-0.15, 0.15)

        vf = (
            f"trim=start={trim_start},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
            f"crop=iw-{crop*2}:ih-{crop*2},"
            f"scale=720:1280:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
            f"pad=720:1280:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2:color=black,"
            f"setpts={1/speed}*PTS"
        )

        def _build_cmd(with_audio: bool):
            cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
                   "-i", src, "-vf", vf]

            if with_audio and audio:
                audio_speed = speed * (2 ** (audio_semitones / 12))
                af = (
                    f"atrim=start={trim_start},asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
                    f"asetrate=44100*{2**(audio_semitones/12)},aresample=44100,"
                    f"atempo={audio_speed / (2**(audio_semitones/12))}"
                )
                cmd += ["-af", af, "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "96k"]
            else:
                cmd += ["-an"]

            meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
            cmd += [
                "-c:v", "libx264",
                "-preset", "veryfast",
                "-crf", "26",
                "-profile:v", "high",
                "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
                "-g", str(random.choice([48, 60, 72])),
                "-t", "60",
                "-map_metadata", "-1",
                "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
                "-metadata", f"comment={meta['comment']}",
                "-metadata", f"title={meta['title']}",
                "-metadata", f"software={meta['software']}",
                "-movflags", "+faststart",
                dst,
            ]
            return cmd

        # первая попытка с аудио
        try:
            ReelUniquifier._run_ffmpeg(_build_cmd(with_audio=True), "universal")
            return dst
        except RuntimeError as e:
            msg = str(e).lower()
            # fallback: если проблема в аудио — пробуем без него
            if "stream" in msg or "audio" in msg or "atrim" in msg or "atempo" in msg:
                print(f"[uniquify] retry без аудио (source без audio track?)")
                try:
                    ReelUniquifier._run_ffmpeg(_build_cmd(with_audio=False), "universal-noaudio")
                    return dst
                except RuntimeError:
                    raise
            raise
    @staticmethod
    def _compact(src, dst, audio):
        """
        МАКСИМАЛЬНАЯ экономия трафика.
        - 720x1280
        - CRF 28 (заметно ниже качество на big screens, но идеально для mobile IG)
        - superfast preset
        - Audio 64k mono (говорильные видео)
        - Итог: файл ~1.5-2.5 MB на 15-сек рилс (~85% экономия)
        
        Использовать если: очень дорогие прокси, короткие рилсы (5-15 сек),
        контент с речью (без сложной музыки).
        """
        speed = random.uniform(0.99, 1.01)
        trim_start = random.uniform(0.10, 0.20)
        crop = random.randint(2, 5)

        vf = (
            f"trim=start={trim_start},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
            f"crop=iw-{crop*2}:ih-{crop*2},"
            f"scale=720:1280:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
            f"pad=720:1280:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2:color=black,"
            f"setpts={1/speed}*PTS"
        )

        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
               "-i", src, "-vf", vf]

        if audio:
            # моно + низкий bitrate — максимум экономии
            cmd += [
                "-af", f"atrim=start={trim_start},asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,atempo={speed},aresample=44100",
                "-c:a", "aac",
                "-b:a", "64k",
                "-ac", "1",   # моно
            ]
        else:
            cmd += ["-an"]

        meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
        cmd += [
            "-c:v", "libx264",
            "-preset", "superfast",
            "-crf", "28",
            "-profile:v", "main",
            "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
            "-g", "60",
            "-t", "60",
            "-map_metadata", "-1",
            "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            dst,
        ]
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)
        return dst
    # ─────────────────────────────────────
    @staticmethod
    def _light(src, dst, audio):
        """Universal - минус trim (мягче для короткого контента)."""
        speed = random.uniform(0.995, 1.005)
        crop = random.randint(1, 3)
        brtn = random.uniform(-0.01, 0.01)
        satu = random.uniform(0.99, 1.01)

        vf = (
            f"crop=iw-{crop*2}:ih-{crop*2},"
            f"eq=brightness={brtn}:saturation={satu},"
            f"setpts={1/speed}*PTS"
        )
        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
               "-i", src, "-vf", vf]

        if audio:
            cmd += ["-af", f"atempo={speed}", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k"]
        else:
            cmd += ["-an"]

        meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
        cmd += [
            "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "23",
            "-g", "60", "-map_metadata", "-1",
            "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
            "-metadata", f"comment={meta['comment']}",
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            dst,
        ]
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)
        return dst

    # ─────────────────────────────────────
    @staticmethod
    def _medium(src, dst, audio):
        """Universal + eq + rotate. Заметно медленнее."""
        speed = random.uniform(0.98, 1.02)
        trim_start = random.uniform(0.15, 0.30)
        crop = random.randint(3, 8)
        rot = random.uniform(-0.4, 0.4)
        brtn = random.uniform(-0.03, 0.03)
        contr = random.uniform(0.97, 1.03)
        satu = random.uniform(0.95, 1.05)
        audio_semitones = random.uniform(-0.2, 0.2)

        vf = (
            f"trim=start={trim_start},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
            f"crop=iw-{crop*2}:ih-{crop*2},"
            f"eq=brightness={brtn}:contrast={contr}:saturation={satu},"
            f"rotate={rot}*PI/180:fillcolor=black@0,"
            f"scale=1080:1920:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
            f"pad=1080:1920:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2:color=black,"
            f"setpts={1/speed}*PTS"
        )
        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
               "-i", src, "-vf", vf]

        if audio:
            audio_speed = speed * (2 ** (audio_semitones / 12))
            af = (
                f"atrim=start={trim_start},asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
                f"asetrate=44100*{2**(audio_semitones/12)},aresample=44100,"
                f"atempo={audio_speed / (2**(audio_semitones/12))}"
            )
            cmd += ["-af", af, "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k"]
        else:
            cmd += ["-an"]

        meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
        cmd += [
            "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-crf", "22",
            "-g", "60", "-t", "60", "-map_metadata", "-1",
            "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
            "-metadata", f"comment={meta['comment']}",
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            dst,
        ]
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)
        return dst

    # ─────────────────────────────────────
    @staticmethod
    def _heavy(src, dst, audio):
        """Как medium но с более агрессивными изменениями. Не для 70 потоков."""
        speed = random.uniform(0.97, 1.03)
        trim_start = random.uniform(0.20, 0.40)
        crop = random.randint(5, 12)
        rot = random.uniform(-0.7, 0.7)
        brtn = random.uniform(-0.05, 0.05)
        contr = random.uniform(0.93, 1.07)
        satu = random.uniform(0.92, 1.08)
        audio_semitones = random.uniform(-0.3, 0.3)

        vf = (
            f"trim=start={trim_start},setpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
            f"crop=iw-{crop*2}:ih-{crop*2},"
            f"eq=brightness={brtn}:contrast={contr}:saturation={satu},"
            f"rotate={rot}*PI/180:fillcolor=black@0,"
            f"scale=1080:1920:force_original_aspect_ratio=decrease,"
            f"pad=1080:1920:(ow-iw)/2:(oh-ih)/2:color=black,"
            f"setpts={1/speed}*PTS"
        )
        cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error",
               "-i", src, "-vf", vf]

        if audio:
            audio_speed = speed * (2 ** (audio_semitones / 12))
            af = (
                f"atrim=start={trim_start},asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,"
                f"asetrate=44100*{2**(audio_semitones/12)},aresample=44100,"
                f"atempo={audio_speed / (2**(audio_semitones/12))}"
            )
            cmd += ["-af", af, "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "128k"]
        else:
            cmd += ["-an"]

        meta = ReelUniquifier._random_encoder_signature()
        cmd += [
            "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "veryfast", "-crf", "20",
            "-g", "60", "-t", "60", "-map_metadata", "-1",
            "-metadata", f"encoder={meta['encoder']}",
            "-metadata", f"comment={meta['comment']}",
            "-movflags", "+faststart",
            dst,
        ]
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)
        return dst

core/orchestrator.py
"""
Оркестратор задач с трекингом активных логинов (защита от дублей),
поддержкой first_setup (avatar + bio перед первым заливом).
"""
import asyncio
import random
import hashlib
from asyncio import Semaphore
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path
from core.asset_picker import AssetPicker

from core.image_uniquifier import ImageUniquifier
from core.device import DeviceProfile
from core.session import IGSession
from core.geo import GeoResolver
from core.ig_client import (IGClient, LoginError, UploadError,
                            TwoFactorRequired, CheckpointError)
from core.checkpoint import CheckpointSolver
from core.uniquifier import ReelUniquifier


class Orchestrator:
    # Общий на ВСЕ инстансы: логины что сейчас в работе (защита от дублей запусков)
    _running_logins: set = set()
    _running_lock = None

    def __init__(self, threads: int, on_status, db=None, settings: dict = None,
                 stats_sink=None, geo: GeoResolver = None):
        self.sem = Semaphore(threads)
        self.on_status = on_status
        self.db = db
        self.settings = settings or {}
        self.stats_sink = stats_sink
        self.geo = geo or GeoResolver(db_path="data/geo_cache.db")

    @classmethod
    def _get_lock(cls):
        if cls._running_lock is None:
            cls._running_lock = asyncio.Lock()
        return cls._running_lock

    # ============================================================
    async def _sleep(self, sec: float):
        """
        Замена asyncio.sleep — гоняет time.sleep в thread pool executor'е,
        чтобы qasync НЕ создавал QTimer на каждую паузу.
        Это лечит 'QEventDispatcherWin32::registerTimer: Failed to create a timer'
        при 70+ параллельных потоках.
        """
        if sec <= 0:
            return
        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
        await loop.run_in_executor(None, time.sleep, sec)
    # ============================================================
    async def run_batch(self, accounts: list, action: str, params: dict):
        # фильтруем аккаунты которые уже обрабатываются другой задачей
        async with self._get_lock():
            skipped_logins = []
            actual_accounts = []
            actual_indices = []
            for i, a in enumerate(accounts):
                login = a.get("login", "")
                if login in self._running_logins:
                    skipped_logins.append(login)
                else:
                    self._running_logins.add(login)
                    actual_accounts.append(a)
                    actual_indices.append(i)

        if skipped_logins:
            print(f"[orchestrator] пропущено {len(skipped_logins)} акков "
                  f"— уже в работе другой задачи")

        if not actual_accounts:
            return

        try:
            tasks = [asyncio.create_task(self._run_one(actual_indices[k], a, action, params))
                     for k, a in enumerate(actual_accounts)]
            await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)
        finally:
            async with self._get_lock():
                for a in actual_accounts:
                    self._running_logins.discard(a.get("login", ""))

    # ============================================================
    async def _run_one(self, row, acc, action, params):
        async with self.sem:
            spread = min(60.0, params.get("delay_max", 90))
            await self._sleep(random.uniform(0.3, spread))
            self.on_status(row, "running", "")

            retry = int(params.get("retry", 1))
            for attempt in range(retry + 1):
                try:
                    result = await self._do_action(acc, action, params)
                    self.on_status(row, "success", "")
                    if self.db:
                        try:
                            self.db.log_task_run(acc["login"], action, "success")
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    if action == "collect_stats" and result:
                        # сохраняем в БД для последующей фильтрации мусорных
                        if self.db:
                            try:
                                self.db.update_stats(acc["login"], result)
                            except Exception:
                                pass
                        if self.stats_sink:
                            self.stats_sink(acc["login"], result)
                    return result

                except TwoFactorRequired:
                    self.on_status(row, "error", "2FA required, no totp_secret")
                    if self.db:
                        self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "error", "2FA no secret")
                    return

                except LoginError as e:
                    if attempt < retry:
                        await self._sleep(random.uniform(3, 8))
                        continue
                    self.on_status(row, "banned", f"login: {str(e)[:120]}")
                    if self.db:
                        self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "banned", str(e)[:200])
                    return

                except CheckpointError as e:
                    self.on_status(row, "error", f"checkpoint: {str(e)[:120]}")
                    if self.db:
                        self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "error", f"checkpoint: {str(e)[:200]}")
                    if params.get("rotate_proxy_on_ban") and self.db:
                        new_proxy = self.db.get_free_proxy()
                        if new_proxy and attempt < retry:
                            acc["proxy"] = new_proxy
                            self.db.upsert_account(acc)
                            self.db.bind_proxy(new_proxy, acc["login"])
                            await self._sleep(random.uniform(10, 25))
                            continue
                    return

                except Exception as e:
                    msg = str(e).lower()

                    hard_signals = (
                        "feedback_required", "spam", "action_blocked",
                        "we restrict certain", "biography_length",
                        "url_missing", "cannot_be_uploaded", "invalid",
                        "not_allowed",
                    )
                    is_hard = any(sig in msg for sig in hard_signals)

                    is_proxy = ("407" in msg or "connect tunnel" in msg
                                or "curl: (28)" in msg or "curl: (7)" in msg
                                or "curl: (56)" in msg or "curl: (55)" in msg
                                or "timed out" in msg
                                or "connection reset" in msg or "429" in msg)

                    is_soft_action = ("transcode" in msg or "mismatch" in msg
                                      or "server error" in msg or "500" in msg
                                      or "502" in msg or "503" in msg
                                      or "504" in msg)

                    if is_hard:
                        self.on_status(row, "error", f"hard: {str(e)[:150]}")
                        if self.db:
                            self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "error", f"hard: {str(e)[:200]}")
                        return

                    # ── АВТО-РОТАЦИЯ ПРОКСИ при traffic error ──
                    if is_proxy and params.get("rotate_proxy_on_traffic", True) and self.db:
                        old_proxy = acc.get("proxy")
                        new_proxy = self.db.get_free_proxy()
                        if new_proxy and new_proxy != old_proxy:
                            # освобождаем старую (помечаем как исчерпанную)
                            self.db.conn.execute(
                                "UPDATE proxies SET status='bad', bound_to=NULL WHERE proxy=?",
                                (old_proxy,)
                            )
                            # привязываем новую
                            acc["proxy"] = new_proxy
                            self.db.upsert_account(acc)
                            self.db.bind_proxy(new_proxy, acc["login"])
                            self.db.conn.commit()
                            print(f"[proxy-rotate:{acc['login']}] "
                                  f"exhausted {old_proxy[:30]}... → new {new_proxy[:30]}...")
                            # даём паузу перед retry с новой прокси
                            await self._sleep(random.uniform(3, 8))
                            if attempt < retry:
                                continue

                    if attempt < retry:
                        if is_proxy:
                            await self._sleep(random.uniform(15, 45))
                        elif is_soft_action:
                            await self._sleep(random.uniform(10, 25))
                        else:
                            await self._sleep(random.uniform(3, 8))
                        continue

                    self.on_status(row, "error", str(e)[:200])
                    if self.db:
                        self.db.set_status(acc["login"], "error", str(e)[:200])
                    return

    # ============================================================
    async def _do_action(self, acc, action, params):
        if not acc.get("proxy"):
            raise ValueError("no proxy assigned to account")

        country, tz_offset = await self.geo.resolve(acc["proxy"])

        has_full_device = (
            acc.get("device_id") and acc.get("model") and
            acc.get("ig_version") and acc.get("os_release")
        )
        device = None
        if has_full_device:
            try:
                device = DeviceProfile.from_saved(acc)
            except Exception:
                device = None

        if device is None:
            device = DeviceProfile.generate(
                seed=acc["login"],
                platform=acc.get("platform") or params.get("platform"),
                country_hint=country,
                tz_offset_hint=tz_offset,
            )
            if self.db:
                saved = device.to_saved()
                acc.update({k: v for k, v in saved.items()
                           if k not in ("seed", "waterfall_id", "pigeon_session_id",
                                        "session_id", "mid")})
                self.db.upsert_account(acc)

        if country and country != device.country:
            device.country = country
            from assets.locale_map import pick_timezone_for_locale
            device.timezone_offset = pick_timezone_for_locale(device.locale, tz_offset)

        # ленивая десериализация куков — не парсим все 20k на старте
        cookies = acc.get("cookies")
        if not cookies and acc.get("cookies_json"):
            try:
                cookies = json.loads(acc["cookies_json"])
                acc["cookies"] = cookies   # кэшируем для повторных обращений
            except Exception:
                cookies = None

        sess = IGSession(device, acc["proxy"], cookies)
        cli = IGClient(sess, acc)

        try:
            # Login (с обработкой чекпоинта)
            try:
                uid = await cli.login()
            except CheckpointError as ce:
                solver = CheckpointSolver(cli, acc, self.settings)
                payload = ce.args[0] if ce.args else {}
                if await solver.solve(payload):
                    uid = await cli.login()
                else:
                    raise

            # Прогрев только для password-flow (без куков)
            if not acc.get("cookies"):
                try:
                    await cli.warm_qe_sync(str(uid))
                    await self._sleep(random.uniform(1.5, 3.0))
                except Exception:
                    pass

            # Целевое действие
            if action == "upload_reel":
                # 1. Проверка приватности — только если ещё не верифицировали
                if params.get("ensure_public", True) and not acc.get("is_public_verified"):
                    try:
                        status = await cli.ensure_public(str(uid))
                        if status == "made_public":
                            print(f"[privacy:{acc['login']}] был приватным — открыт")
                            await self._sleep(random.uniform(3, 7))
                        # успешная проверка — запоминаем чтобы больше не дёргать
                        if status in ("was_public", "made_public") and self.db:
                            self.db.mark_public_verified(acc["login"])
                            acc["is_public_verified"] = 1
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"[privacy:{acc['login']}] не удалось открыть: {e}")

                # 2. First setup — био и/или аватар перед первым заливом
                need_setup = (
                    params.get("first_setup_enable") or
                    params.get("first_setup_avatar")
                )
                if need_setup and not acc.get("first_setup_done"):
                    await self._do_first_setup(cli, acc, params)
                return await self._upload(cli, params)

            if action == "set_bio":
                if params.get("ensure_public", False) and not acc.get("is_public_verified"):
                    try:
                        status = await cli.ensure_public(str(uid))
                        if status in ("was_public", "made_public") and self.db:
                            self.db.mark_public_verified(acc["login"])
                            acc["is_public_verified"] = 1
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                bio_text = self._pick_bio(params, acc["login"])
                result = await cli.set_biography(bio_text)
                if self.db and result.get("status") == "ok":
                    h = hashlib.md5(bio_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
                    self.db.set_bio_hash(acc["login"], h)
                return result
                
            if action == "set_avatar":
                if params.get("ensure_public", False) and not acc.get("is_public_verified"):
                    try:
                        status = await cli.ensure_public(str(uid))
                        if status in ("was_public", "made_public") and self.db:
                            self.db.mark_public_verified(acc["login"])
                            acc["is_public_verified"] = 1
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                return await self._do_avatar_only(cli, acc, params)
            if action == "collect_stats":
                return await cli.get_self_stats(str(uid))

            raise ValueError(f"unknown action: {action}")

        finally:
            # сохраняем свежие куки после ЛЮБОГО действия
            try:
                if self.db:
                    fresh_cookies = {c.name: c.value for c in sess._session.cookies.jar}
                    if sess._auth_header:
                        fresh_cookies["Authorization"] = sess._auth_header
                    if sess.mid:
                        fresh_cookies["X-MID"] = sess.mid
                    if fresh_cookies.get("sessionid"):
                        self.db.save_cookies(acc["login"], fresh_cookies)
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                await sess.close()
            except Exception:
                pass

    # ============================================================
    async def _do_first_setup(self, cli, acc, params):
        """
        Ставит био и/или аватарку перед первым заливом рилса.
        Умные задержки: fresh акки (без рилсов) — осторожнее, warmed — быстрее.
        """
        import hashlib
        login = acc.get("login", "?")

        do_bio = params.get("first_setup_enable", False)
        do_avatar = params.get("first_setup_avatar", False)
        consume_used = params.get("consume_used_assets", True)

        # ── определяем "чувствительность" акка ──
        # fresh = ни одного залитого рилса + без предыдущих ошибок
        is_fresh = (
            not acc.get("reels_count") or int(acc.get("reels_count", 0)) == 0
        ) or acc.get("last_error")   # ← ИЛИ, не И, и без "не"

        if is_fresh:
            avatar_delay = (8, 18)   # осторожнее для новых
            bio_delay = (10, 22)
        else:
            avatar_delay = (4, 10)   # быстрее для проверенных
            bio_delay = (5, 12)

        # ─── 1. Аватар ───
        if do_avatar:
            avatar_folder = params.get("avatar_folder", "")
            if avatar_folder and Path(avatar_folder).is_dir():
                # выбор файла — с резервом или без, в зависимости от consume_used
                reserved_path = None
                picked_path = None

                if consume_used:
                    reserved_path = await AssetPicker.pick(avatar_folder)
                    if reserved_path is None:
                        print(f"[first_setup:{login}] avatar folder empty (all used)")
                        picked_path = None
                    else:
                        picked_path = reserved_path
                else:
                    avatars = []
                    for ext in ("*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.png", "*.webp",
                                "*.JPG", "*.JPEG", "*.PNG", "*.WEBP"):
                        avatars.extend(Path(avatar_folder).glob(ext))
                    avatars = [a for a in avatars if not a.name.endswith(".taken")]
                    if avatars:
                        picked_path = str(random.choice(avatars))
                    else:
                        print(f"[first_setup:{login}] avatar folder empty")

                if picked_path:
                    try:
                        with open(picked_path, "rb") as f:
                            new_avatar_hash = hashlib.md5(f.read()).hexdigest()

                        if acc.get("avatar_hash") == new_avatar_hash:
                            if reserved_path:
                                AssetPicker.release(reserved_path)
                        else:
                            # уникализация под каждый акк
                            upload_path = picked_path
                            temp_uniq = None
                            if params.get("uniquify_avatars", True):
                                temp_uniq = ImageUniquifier.unique(picked_path, is_avatar=True)
                                upload_path = temp_uniq

                            try:
                                await cli.change_profile_picture(upload_path)
                            finally:
                                if temp_uniq:
                                    try:
                                        Path(temp_uniq).unlink(missing_ok=True)
                                    except Exception:
                                        pass

                            if self.db:
                                self.db.conn.execute(
                                    "UPDATE accounts SET avatar_hash=?, "
                                    "avatar_updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE login=?",
                                    (new_avatar_hash, login)
                                )
                                self.db.conn.commit()

                            if reserved_path and consume_used:
                                AssetPicker.consume(reserved_path)
                                print(f"[first_setup:{login}] avatar consumed: "
                                      f"{Path(reserved_path).name[:-6]}")
                            elif reserved_path:
                                AssetPicker.release(reserved_path)

                            await self._sleep(random.uniform(*avatar_delay))
                    except Exception as e:
                        if reserved_path:
                            AssetPicker.release(reserved_path)
                        print(f"[first_setup:{login}] avatar fail: {e}")

    # ─── 2. Био ───
        if do_bio:
            try:
                import json
                bio_path = Path("data/bio_settings.json")
                if bio_path.exists():
                    bio_cfg = json.loads(bio_path.read_text("utf-8"))
                    bio_text = bio_cfg.get("bio", "").strip()
                    if bio_text:
                        # ── ЖЁСТКАЯ защита: если био когда-либо ставили — не трогаем ──
                        if params.get("skip_if_ever_set_bio", True) and acc.get("bio_hash"):
                            print(f"[first_setup:{login}] bio уже ставили ранее — пропуск")
                        else:
                            # разбиваем на варианты по '-' на отдельной строке
                            variants = self._split_variants(bio_text)
                            if len(variants) > 1:
                                idx = int(hashlib.md5(login.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:8], 16) % len(variants)
                                chosen_bio = variants[idx]
                            elif variants:
                                chosen_bio = variants[0]
                            else:
                                chosen_bio = bio_text

                            bio_final = self._spin(chosen_bio)

                            # защита от длинной строки
                            if len(bio_final) > 150:
                                print(f"[first_setup:{login}] bio too long ({len(bio_final)} chars) — обрезаю")
                                bio_final = bio_final[:150]

                            new_bio_hash = hashlib.md5(bio_final.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
                            # МЯГКАЯ проверка: если хэш совпал — пропуск
                            if acc.get("bio_hash") == new_bio_hash:
                                pass
                            else:
                                result = await cli.set_biography(bio_final)
                                if result.get("status") == "ok" and self.db:
                                    self.db.set_bio_hash(login, new_bio_hash)
                                await self._sleep(random.uniform(*bio_delay))
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[first_setup:{login}] bio fail: {e}")

        if (do_bio or do_avatar) and self.db:
            try:
                self.db.mark_setup_done(login)
                acc["first_setup_done"] = 1
            except Exception:
                pass
    async def _do_avatar_only(self, cli, acc, params):
        """Задача только на смену аватарки. Атомарный резерв + удаление ТОЛЬКО при consume_used."""
        import hashlib
        login = acc.get("login", "?")

        avatar_folder = params.get("avatar_folder", "")
        if not avatar_folder or not Path(avatar_folder).is_dir():
            raise ValueError("avatar_folder not set or does not exist")

        consume_used = params.get("consume_used_assets", True)

        # Проверка на IG — до резерва файла
        if params.get("skip_if_set"):
            uid_str = ""
            try:
                for c in cli.s._session.cookies.jar:
                    if c.name == "ds_user_id":
                        uid_str = c.value
                        break
                if uid_str:
                    r = await cli.s.request(
                        "GET", f"{cli.BASE}/api/v1/users/{uid_str}/info/"
                    )
                    j = r.json()
                    if j.get("status") == "ok" and j.get("user"):
                        has_avatar = not j["user"].get("has_anonymous_profile_picture", True)
                        if has_avatar:
                            return {"status": "skipped", "reason": "avatar already set on IG"}
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[avatar:{login}] info fetch fail: {e}")

        # выбор файла: с резервом или без
        reserved_path = None
        picked_path = None

        if consume_used:
            reserved_path = await AssetPicker.pick(avatar_folder)
            if reserved_path is None:
                raise ValueError(f"no available images in {avatar_folder} (all consumed)")
            picked_path = reserved_path
        else:
            avatars = []
            for ext in ("*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.png", "*.webp",
                        "*.JPG", "*.JPEG", "*.PNG", "*.WEBP"):
                avatars.extend(Path(avatar_folder).glob(ext))
            avatars = [a for a in avatars if not a.name.endswith(".taken")]
            if not avatars:
                raise ValueError(f"no images in {avatar_folder}")
            picked_path = str(random.choice(avatars))

        try:
            with open(picked_path, "rb") as f:
                new_hash = hashlib.md5(f.read()).hexdigest()

            await self._sleep(random.uniform(
                params.get("delay_min", 15),
                params.get("delay_max", 60),
            ))

            upload_path = picked_path
            temp_uniq = None
            if params.get("uniquify_avatars", True):
                temp_uniq = ImageUniquifier.unique(picked_path, is_avatar=True)
                upload_path = temp_uniq

            try:
                result = await cli.change_profile_picture(upload_path)
            finally:
                if temp_uniq:
                    try:
                        Path(temp_uniq).unlink(missing_ok=True)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

            if self.db:
                try:
                    self.db.conn.execute(
                        "UPDATE accounts SET avatar_hash=?, avatar_updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
                        "WHERE login=?", (new_hash, login)
                    )
                    if params.get("mark_setup"):
                        self.db.conn.execute(
                            "UPDATE accounts SET first_setup_done=1 WHERE login=?", (login,)
                        )
                    self.db.conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if reserved_path and consume_used:
                AssetPicker.consume(reserved_path)
                print(f"[avatar:{login}] consumed: {Path(reserved_path).name[:-6]}")
            elif reserved_path:
                AssetPicker.release(reserved_path)
            return result

        except Exception:
            if reserved_path:
                AssetPicker.release(reserved_path)
            raise

    # ============================================================
    async def _upload(self, cli: IGClient, params: dict):
        # 1. Видео
        src = params.get("video_path")
        if not src and params.get("video_folder"):
            folder = Path(params["video_folder"])
            files = (list(folder.glob("*.mp4")) + list(folder.glob("*.mov")) +
                     list(folder.glob("*.m4v")) + list(folder.glob("*.mkv")))
            if not files:
                raise ValueError("empty video folder")
            src = str(random.choice(files))
        if not src:
            raise ValueError("no video source")
        if not Path(src).exists():
            raise ValueError(f"video not found: {src}")

        # 2. Обложка — с резервом или без в зависимости от consume_covers
        custom_cover = None
        cover_reserved: str | None = None
        cover_path = params.get("cover_path", "")
        cover_folder = params.get("cover_folder", "")
        consume_cover = params.get("consume_covers", True)
        temp_cover_uniq: str | None = None

        if cover_path and Path(cover_path).exists():
            # разовый файл
            if params.get("uniquify_covers", True):
                temp_cover_uniq = await ImageUniquifier.unique_async(cover_path, is_avatar=False)
                custom_cover = temp_cover_uniq
            else:
                custom_cover = cover_path

        elif cover_folder and Path(cover_folder).is_dir():
            if consume_cover:
                # атомарный резерв + удаление
                cover_reserved = await AssetPicker.pick(cover_folder)
                if cover_reserved:
                    if params.get("uniquify_covers", True):
                        temp_cover_uniq = await ImageUniquifier.unique_async(cover_reserved, is_avatar=False)
                        custom_cover = temp_cover_uniq
                    else:
                        custom_cover = cover_reserved
                else:
                    print(f"[upload:{cli.acc.get('login')}] no cover left")
            else:
                # ВЕРИФНУТЫЙ РЕЖИМ: рандом по кругу, файлы не трогаем
                covers = []
                for ext in ("*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.png", "*.webp",
                            "*.JPG", "*.JPEG", "*.PNG", "*.WEBP"):
                    covers.extend(Path(cover_folder).glob(ext))
                covers = [c for c in covers if not c.name.endswith(".taken")]
                if covers:
                    chosen = str(random.choice(covers))
                    if params.get("uniquify_covers", True):
                        temp_cover_uniq = await ImageUniquifier.unique_async(chosen, is_avatar=False)
                        custom_cover = temp_cover_uniq
                    else:
                        custom_cover = chosen
                else:
                    print(f"[upload:{cli.acc.get('login')}] no covers in folder")

        # 3. Уникализация видео
        if params.get("uniquify"):
            level = params.get("uniquify_level", "universal")
            audio = params.get("uniquify_audio", True)
            
            orig_size = Path(src).stat().st_size
            src = await ReelUniquifier.unique_async(src, level=level, audio=audio)
            new_size = Path(src).stat().st_size
            
            saving_pct = int((1 - new_size / orig_size) * 100) if orig_size > 0 else 0
            print(f"[uniquify:{cli.acc.get('login')}] "
                  f"{orig_size // 1024}KB → {new_size // 1024}KB "
                  f"(экономия {saving_pct}%)")

        # 4. Caption
        raw_caption = params.get("caption", "")
        variants = self._split_variants(raw_caption)
        if variants:
            picked = random.choice(variants)
        else:
            picked = raw_caption
        caption = self._spin(picked)
        # ── считаем hash + preview выбранного caption для аналитики ──
        import hashlib as _hl
        caption_hash = _hl.md5(caption.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() if caption else None
        caption_preview = (caption[:200] + "...") if len(caption) > 200 else caption
        # 5. Human delay
        await self._sleep(random.uniform(
            params.get("delay_min", 25),
            params.get("delay_max", 90)
        ))

        # 6. Публикация
        try:
            result = await cli.upload_reel(
                src,
                caption=caption,
                share_to_feed=params.get("share_to_feed", False),
                disable_comments=params.get("disable_comments", False),
                custom_cover_path=custom_cover,
            )
            if temp_cover_uniq:
                try:
                    Path(temp_cover_uniq).unlink(missing_ok=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        except Exception:
            if cover_reserved:
                AssetPicker.release(cover_reserved)
            if temp_cover_uniq:
                try:
                    Path(temp_cover_uniq).unlink(missing_ok=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            raise

        # успех — cover сжигаем ТОЛЬКО если consume_cover=True
        if cover_reserved:
            if consume_cover:
                AssetPicker.consume(cover_reserved)
                print(f"[upload:{cli.acc.get('login')}] cover consumed: "
                      f"{Path(cover_reserved).name[:-6]}")
            else:
                AssetPicker.release(cover_reserved)

        if self.db and result.get("media"):
            cover_used = ""
            if cover_reserved:
                cover_used = Path(cover_reserved).name
                if cover_used.endswith(".taken"):
                    cover_used = cover_used[:-6]
            elif custom_cover:
                cover_used = Path(custom_cover).name

            self.db.log_upload(
                cli.acc["login"],
                result["media"].get("id", ""),
                result["media"].get("code", ""),
                cover_filename=cover_used,
                caption_hash=caption_hash,
                caption_preview=caption_preview,
            )
        return result

    # ============================================================
    @staticmethod
    def _spin(text: str) -> str:
        """Раскрытие {a|b|c} спинтакса."""
        import re
        def repl(m):
            return random.choice(m.group(1).split("|"))
        prev = None
        while prev != text:
            prev = text
            text = re.sub(r"\{([^{}]+)\}", repl, text)
        return text
    @staticmethod
    def _split_variants(text: str) -> list[str]:
        """
        Разбивает текст на варианты по строкам-разделителям (только '-' или '---'
        на отдельной строке). Возвращает список непустых блоков.
        Если разделителей нет — вернёт [весь_текст].
        """
        import re
        if not text:
            return []
        blocks = []
        current = []
        for line in text.split("\n"):
            if re.match(r"^\s*-+\s*$", line):
                if current:
                    blocks.append("\n".join(current).strip())
                    current = []
            else:
                current.append(line)
        if current:
            blocks.append("\n".join(current).strip())
        return [b for b in blocks if b]
    def _pick_bio(self, params: dict, login: str) -> str:
        """
        Био: если несколько вариантов через '-' на отдельной строке —
        выбирается СТАБИЛЬНО по логину (md5-hash, устойчив к перезапускам).
        Один акк = одно био всегда, при повторных запусках то же самое.
        """
        import hashlib

        bio = params.get("bio", "")
        variants = self._split_variants(bio)

        def _stable_index(text: str, n: int) -> int:
            """md5-хэш логина → индекс 0..n-1. Не зависит от PYTHONHASHSEED."""
            h = int(hashlib.md5(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:8], 16)
            return h % n

        if len(variants) > 1:
            chosen = variants[_stable_index(login, len(variants))]
        elif variants:
            chosen = variants[0]
        else:
            chosen = bio

        # legacy режим multiline (если галка стоит и нет '-')
        if params.get("multiline") and len(variants) <= 1:
            lines = [l for l in chosen.splitlines() if l.strip()]
            if lines:
                chosen = lines[_stable_index(login, len(lines))]

        return self._spin(chosen)

core/image_uniquifier.py
"""
Уникализатор картинок для аватарок / covers.
Ломает pHash + PhotoDNA + чистит EXIF + меняет file-level сигнатуру.

Что делаем:
  1. Открываем через PIL → полностью пересобираем pixel-data (ломает CRC/hash файла)
  2. Убираем ВСЕ EXIF/ICC (Meta не любит пустой EXIF, поэтому ставим фейковый iPhone/Samsung)
  3. Micro-crop 1-4px по краям → сдвиг pHash-грида
  4. Резайз ±1-3% → ломает DCT-коэффициенты
  5. Micro-rotation ±0.3° → ломает симметричные фингерпринты
  6. Легкая коррекция brightness/contrast/saturation ±1-2%
  7. Добавляем PIL-noise на 1-2% пикселей (imperceptible visually)
  8. JPEG re-encode с случайным quality 82-91 → ломает JPEG DCT-hash
  9. Случайные размеры финального файла (padding до 320/640/720/1080 px)

Не искажает картинку заметно для глаза, но каждый выход = уникальный fingerprint.
"""
import io
import random
import tempfile
import asyncio
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from PIL import Image, ImageEnhance, ImageOps


# фейковые EXIF-профили (подставляются вместо пустого/палевного EXIF)
_FAKE_EXIF_PROFILES = [
    {
        "Make": "Apple",
        "Model": "iPhone 15 Pro",
        "Software": "17.5.1",
        "LensModel": "iPhone 15 Pro back triple camera 6.86mm f/1.78",
    },
    {
        "Make": "Apple",
        "Model": "iPhone 14",
        "Software": "17.4",
        "LensModel": "iPhone 14 back dual wide camera 5.7mm f/1.5",
    },
    {
        "Make": "samsung",
        "Model": "SM-S928B",
        "Software": "S928BXXU5CXIA",
    },
    {
        "Make": "samsung",
        "Model": "SM-S918B",
        "Software": "S918BXXUAFXIA",
    },
    {
        "Make": "Google",
        "Model": "Pixel 8 Pro",
        "Software": "HG4ADB.240705.001",
    },
    {
        "Make": "Xiaomi",
        "Model": "23117RA68G",
        "Software": "V816.0.9.0.UNRMIXM",
    },
]


class ImageUniquifier:

    @staticmethod
    def unique(src: str,
               dst: str | None = None,
               target_size: int | None = None,
               is_avatar: bool = False) -> str:
        """
        Обрабатывает картинку.
        :param src: путь к оригиналу
        :param dst: куда сохранить (по умолчанию — tmp)
        :param target_size: если задан — финальный размер (квадрат) в px
        :param is_avatar: аватары IG хочет квадратные, cover'ы 9:16
        :return: путь к уникализированной картинке
        """
        dst = dst or tempfile.mktemp(suffix=".jpg")

        img = Image.open(src).convert("RGB")

        # 1. Micro-crop 1-4 px по каждой стороне (rand на каждую сторону отдельно)
        w, h = img.size
        left = random.randint(1, 4)
        top = random.randint(1, 4)
        right = w - random.randint(1, 4)
        bottom = h - random.randint(1, 4)
        img = img.crop((left, top, right, bottom))

        # 2. Micro-rotation ±0.3° (imperceptible)
        angle = random.uniform(-0.3, 0.3)
        img = img.rotate(angle, resample=Image.BICUBIC, fillcolor=(0, 0, 0))

        # 3. Финальный размер — под целевой формат
        if is_avatar:
            # аватарка IG — квадрат
            side = target_size or random.choice([320, 360, 400, 480, 512, 640, 720, 800])
            img = ImageOps.fit(img, (side, side), method=Image.LANCZOS)
        else:
            # cover — вертикаль 9:16
            base_w = target_size or random.choice([720, 810, 900, 1080])
            base_h = int(base_w * 16 / 9)
            img = ImageOps.fit(img, (base_w, base_h), method=Image.LANCZOS)

        # 4. Резайз ±1-3% и обратно к целевому — ломает DCT
        w, h = img.size
        drift = random.uniform(0.98, 1.03)
        img = img.resize(
            (int(w * drift), int(h * drift)),
            resample=Image.LANCZOS
        )
        img = img.resize((w, h), resample=Image.LANCZOS)

        # 5. Легкая коррекция цвета
        img = ImageEnhance.Brightness(img).enhance(random.uniform(0.98, 1.02))
        img = ImageEnhance.Contrast(img).enhance(random.uniform(0.98, 1.02))
        img = ImageEnhance.Color(img).enhance(random.uniform(0.97, 1.03))
        img = ImageEnhance.Sharpness(img).enhance(random.uniform(0.95, 1.05))

        # 6. Micro-noise на случайных пикселях (~0.5-1% пикселей)
        # ломает pixel-hash не касаясь визуального восприятия
        pixels = img.load()
        w, h = img.size
        noise_count = int(w * h * random.uniform(0.005, 0.010))
        for _ in range(noise_count):
            x = random.randint(0, w - 1)
            y = random.randint(0, h - 1)
            r, g, b = pixels[x, y]
            dr = random.randint(-3, 3)
            dg = random.randint(-3, 3)
            db = random.randint(-3, 3)
            pixels[x, y] = (
                max(0, min(255, r + dr)),
                max(0, min(255, g + dg)),
                max(0, min(255, b + db)),
            )

        # 7. JPEG re-encode с random quality + фейковый EXIF
        quality = random.randint(82, 91)
        exif_bytes = ImageUniquifier._build_fake_exif()

        img.save(
            dst,
            "JPEG",
            quality=quality,
            optimize=True,
            progressive=random.choice([True, False]),
            exif=exif_bytes,
            subsampling=random.choice([0, 1, 2]),  # 4:4:4 / 4:2:2 / 4:2:0
        )
        return dst
# ================================================================
    @staticmethod
    async def unique_async(src: str,
                           dst: str | None = None,
                           target_size: int | None = None,
                           is_avatar: bool = False) -> str:
        """Асинхронная обёртка — запускает unique() в thread pool."""
        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
        return await loop.run_in_executor(
            None,  # используем default executor
            ImageUniquifier.unique,
            src, dst, target_size, is_avatar
        )
    # ================================================================
    @staticmethod
    def _build_fake_exif() -> bytes:
        """Собирает минимальный, но реалистичный EXIF-блок под iPhone/Samsung/Pixel."""
        try:
            import piexif
        except ImportError:
            return b""

        profile = random.choice(_FAKE_EXIF_PROFILES)

        # случайная дата съёмки в пределах последних 60 дней
        dt = datetime.now() - timedelta(
            days=random.randint(1, 60),
            hours=random.randint(0, 23),
            minutes=random.randint(0, 59),
        )
        dt_str = dt.strftime("%Y:%m:%d %H:%M:%S")

        zeroth = {
            piexif.ImageIFD.Make: profile["Make"].encode(),
            piexif.ImageIFD.Model: profile["Model"].encode(),
            piexif.ImageIFD.Software: profile["Software"].encode(),
            piexif.ImageIFD.DateTime: dt_str.encode(),
            piexif.ImageIFD.Orientation: 1,
            piexif.ImageIFD.XResolution: (72, 1),
            piexif.ImageIFD.YResolution: (72, 1),
            piexif.ImageIFD.ResolutionUnit: 2,
        }
        exif = {
            piexif.ExifIFD.DateTimeOriginal: dt_str.encode(),
            piexif.ExifIFD.DateTimeDigitized: dt_str.encode(),
            piexif.ExifIFD.ExposureTime: (1, random.randint(30, 250)),
            piexif.ExifIFD.FNumber: (random.choice([15, 18, 22, 28]), 10),
            piexif.ExifIFD.ISOSpeedRatings: random.choice([32, 50, 100, 200, 400]),
            piexif.ExifIFD.FocalLength: (random.choice([26, 28, 35, 50, 57]), 10),
            piexif.ExifIFD.LensModel: profile.get("LensModel", "").encode(),
            piexif.ExifIFD.ColorSpace: 1,
        }

        exif_dict = {
            "0th": zeroth,
            "Exif": exif,
            "1st": {},
            "thumbnail": None,
            "GPS": {},   # намеренно пусто — многие юзеры отключают геометки
        }
        try:
            return piexif.dump(exif_dict)
        except Exception:
            return b""

core/ig_client.py
"""
IGClient — обёртка Instagram Private API. Login/warmup/upload/bio/stats.
"""
import asyncio, json, time, hashlib, random, subprocess, tempfile
from pathlib import Path
import uuid

class LoginError(Exception): pass
class UploadError(Exception): pass
class TwoFactorRequired(Exception): pass
class CheckpointError(Exception): pass


class IGClient:
    BASE = "https://i.instagram.com"

    def __init__(self, session, account):
        self.s = session
        self.acc = account

    # ─────────────── PRELOGIN ───────────────
    async def warm_launcher(self):
        await self.s.request(
            "POST", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/launcher/mobileconfig/",
            data={
                "bool_opt_policy": "0",
                "mobileconfigsessionless": "",
                "api_version": "3",
                "unit_type": "1",
                "ts": str(int(time.time())),
            })

    async def warm_qe_sync(self, uid=None):
        await self.s.request(
            "POST", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/qe/sync/",
            data={
                "id": uid or self.s.device.device_id,
                "server_config_retrieval": "1",
                "experiments": "ig_android_login_identifier_fuzzy_match",
            })

    async def warm_contact_prefill(self):
        await self.s.request(
            "POST", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/accounts/contact_point_prefill/",
            data={"phone_id": self.s.device.phone_id, "usage": "prefill"})

    async def warm_full_prelogin(self):
        await self.warm_launcher()
        await self._pause(0.4, 0.9)
        await self.warm_qe_sync()
        await self._pause(0.3, 0.7)
        await self.warm_contact_prefill()
        await self._pause(0.5, 1.1)

    # ─────────────── LOGIN ───────────────
    async def login(self):
        """Если есть куки → валидируем без /login/. Иначе — полный flow."""

        # ─── путь через куки/токен ───
        if self.acc.get("cookies") or self.s._auth_header:
            try:
                r = await self.s.request(
                    "GET", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/accounts/current_user/"
                )
                try:
                    j = r.json()
                except Exception:
                    j = {}

                # 200 + user → всё ок
                if r.status_code == 200 and j.get("status") == "ok" and j.get("user"):
                    return j["user"]["pk"]

                # 400 + challenge_required — часто suspended/banned
                if r.status_code == 400 and j.get("message") == "challenge_required":
                    challenge = j.get("challenge", {})
                    url = challenge.get("url", "")
                    if "suspended" in url or "disabled" in url:
                        raise LoginError(f"account_suspended: {url}")
                    raise CheckpointError(j)

                # 403 + login_required — сессия умерла
                if r.status_code == 403 and j.get("message") == "login_required":
                    # проваливаемся в password-flow ниже
                    pass
                else:
                    # неожиданный ответ на current_user — тоже фоллбэк
                    pass

            except (LoginError, CheckpointError):
                raise
            except Exception:
                pass  # молча падаем в password

        # ─── password-flow ───
        await self.warm_full_prelogin()

        import time
        ts = int(time.time())
        enc_pwd = f"#PWD_INSTAGRAM:0:{ts}:{self.acc['password']}"
        dev = self.s.device

        payload = {
            "jazoest": "2" + str(sum(ord(c) for c in dev.phone_id)),
            "country_codes": '[{"country_code":"1","source":["default"]}]',
            "phone_id": dev.phone_id,
            "enc_password": enc_pwd,
            "username": self.acc["login"],
            "adid": dev.advertising_id,
            "guid": dev.device_id,
            "device_id": dev.android_id or dev.device_id,
            "google_tokens": "[]",
            "login_attempt_count": "0",
        }

        r = await self.s.request(
            "POST",
            f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/accounts/login/",
            data=payload
        )

        try:
            j = r.json()
        except Exception:
            raise LoginError(f"non-JSON: {r.text[:200]}")

        if j.get("two_factor_required"):
            return await self._pass_2fa(j["two_factor_info"])

        if (
            j.get("message") == "checkpoint_required"
            or j.get("error_type", "").startswith("checkpoint")
        ):
            raise CheckpointError(j)

        if j.get("status") != "ok" or not j.get("logged_in_user"):
            raise LoginError(j.get("message") or str(j)[:200])

        return j["logged_in_user"]["pk"]

    async def _pass_2fa(self, info):
        secret = self.acc.get("totp_secret")
        if not secret:
            raise TwoFactorRequired(info)
        import pyotp
        code = pyotp.TOTP(secret).now()
        dev = self.s.device
        r = await self.s.request(
            "POST", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/accounts/two_factor_login/",
            data={
                "verification_code": code,
                "phone_id": dev.phone_id,
                "two_factor_identifier": info["two_factor_identifier"],
                "username": self.acc["login"],
                "trust_signal": "true",
                "device_id": dev.android_id or dev.device_id,
                "guid": dev.device_id,
                "verification_method": "3",
            })
        j = r.json()
        if j.get("status") != "ok":
            raise LoginError(f"2FA failed: {j}")
        return j["logged_in_user"]["pk"]
    
    # ─────────────── WARMUP AFTER LOGIN ───────────────
    async def warm_postlogin(self, uid, intensity="medium"):
        counts = {"min": 3, "medium": 6, "max": 10}.get(intensity, 6)
        endpoints = [
            ("GET",  "/api/v1/feed/timeline/", None),
            ("GET",  "/api/v1/direct_v2/inbox/", None),
            ("GET",  "/api/v1/news/inbox/", None),
            ("GET",  "/api/v1/feed/reels_tray/", None),
            ("GET",  f"/api/v1/users/{uid}/info/", None),
            ("POST", "/api/v1/qe/sync/", {"id": uid, "server_config_retrieval": "1"}),
            ("GET",  "/api/v1/scores/", None),
            ("GET",  "/api/v1/notifications/badge/", None),
            ("GET",  "/api/v1/media/blocked/", None),
            ("GET",  "/api/v1/feed/saved/", None),
        ]
        random.shuffle(endpoints)
        for method, path, data in endpoints[:counts]:
            try:
                await self.s.request(method, self.BASE + path, data=data)
            except Exception:
                pass
            await self._pause(0.6, 2.4)
    async def change_profile_picture(self, image_path: str):
        """
        Двухшаговый flow как в реальном IG Android клиенте:
        1) rupload_igphoto — заливаем фото, получаем upload_id
        2) change_profile_picture — активируем как аватар
        """
        from pathlib import Path
        import time as _t
        import json as _json

        if not Path(image_path).exists():
            raise UploadError(f"avatar not found: {image_path}")

        with open(image_path, "rb") as f:
            img_bytes = f.read()

        upload_id = str(int(_t.time() * 1000))
        dev = self.s.device
        entity_name = f"{upload_id}_0_{random.randint(1_000_000_000, 9_999_999_999)}"

        # ─── Шаг 1: rupload_igphoto ───
        rupload_params = {
            "media_type": "1",
            "upload_id": upload_id,
            "image_compression": _json.dumps({
                "lib_name": "moz",
                "lib_version": "3.1.m",
                "quality": "80",
            }),
            "retry_context": '{"num_step_auto_retry":0,"num_reupload":0,"num_step_manual_retry":0}',
            "xsharing_user_ids": "[]",
        }

        headers = {
            "Offset": "0",
            "X-Entity-Length": str(len(img_bytes)),
            "X-Entity-Name": entity_name,
            "X-Entity-Type": "image/jpeg",
            "X-Instagram-Rupload-Params": _json.dumps(rupload_params),
            "Content-Type": "application/octet-stream",
        }

        r = await self.s.request(
            "POST",
            f"{self.BASE}/rupload_igphoto/{entity_name}",
            data=img_bytes,
            headers=headers,
            timeout=60,
        )
        if r.status_code != 200:
            raise UploadError(f"photo rupload: HTTP {r.status_code} {r.text[:200]}")

        # даём серверу время обработать
        await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(1.5, 3.0))

        # ─── Шаг 2: change_profile_picture ───
        from curl_cffi import CurlMime
        mp = CurlMime()
        mp.addpart(name="_uuid",
                   content_type="text/plain; charset=utf-8",
                   data=dev.device_id.encode("utf-8"))
        mp.addpart(name="use_fbuploader",
                   content_type="text/plain; charset=utf-8",
                   data=b"true")
        mp.addpart(name="upload_id",
                   content_type="text/plain; charset=utf-8",
                   data=upload_id.encode("utf-8"))

        r = await self.s.request(
            "POST",
            f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/accounts/change_profile_picture/",
            multipart=mp,
        )

        try:
            j = r.json()
        except Exception:
            raise UploadError(
                f"change_pfp non-JSON HTTP {r.status_code}: {r.text[:200]}"
            )

        if j.get("status") != "ok":
            raise UploadError(f"change_pfp failed: {j.get('message', str(j)[:200])}")
        return j
# ─────────────── PRIVACY ───────────────
    async def get_privacy_status(self, user_id: str) -> bool | None:
        """
        Возвращает True если аккаунт приватный, False если публичный, None если не удалось.
        """
        try:
            r = await self.s.request(
                "GET", f"{self.BASE}/api/v1/users/{user_id}/info/"
            )
            j = r.json()
            if j.get("status") == "ok" and j.get("user"):
                return bool(j["user"].get("is_private", False))
        except Exception:
            pass
        return None

    async def set_privacy(self, public: bool) -> dict:
        """
        Меняет приватность аккаунта.
        :param public: True = сделать публичным, False = сделать приватным
